### [1] [Robin counting functions on cuboids in the critical scaling regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02297)
*Matthias Baur,Simon Larson*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究长方体上Robin Laplace算子的特征值计数函数，其中Robin参数与谱截断λ相互耦合，重点关注临界区域（Robin参数与√λ成正比）。作者获得了该耦合设置下计数函数的二项渐近展开，并建立了临界区域外的Dirichlet/Neumann渐近恢复以及Pólya型不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Robin边界条件下Laplace算子特征值计数函数的渐近行为，特别是在Robin参数与谱参数λ耦合的临界区域中，第二项渐近系数如何依赖比例常数，以及该行为如何与Dirichlet和Neumann极限相关联。

Method: 通过将Robin参数设定为与√λ成比例（临界区域），分析长方体区域上Robin-Laplace算子特征值计数函数的二项渐近展开；通过比例常数的变化考察第二项系数的连续插值性质；对超出临界区域的参数范围恢复Dirichlet或Neumann渐近；利用比例常数超过维度相关阈值时证明Pólya型不等式。

Result: 在临界区域中，计数函数的二项渐近展开得到确立，且第二项依赖于比例常数并以非平凡方式连续插值于Dirichlet和Neumann情形对应的第二项；在临界区域之外，Dirichlet或Neumann渐近被恢复；当Robin参数与√λ之比超过依赖于维度的阈值时，Pólya型不等式成立。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了Robin参数与谱参数耦合时特征值计数函数更丰富的渐近结构，第二项系数在临界区域中体现出对Robin参数的连续依赖，并在参数超过阈值时保证Pólya型不等式成立，这为理解Robin边界条件对谱渐近的影响提供了完整的图景。

Abstract: We consider eigenvalue counting functions of Robin Laplace operators on cuboids where the Robin parameter and the spectral cut-off $λ$ are coupled. Our main focus is the critical regime, in which the Robin parameter is proportional to $\sqrtλ$. We obtain a two-term asymptotic expansion for the counting functions in this coupled setting. In the critical regime, the second term in the asymptotic expansion depends non-trivially on the proportionality constant and interpolates continuously between the corresponding second terms for the Dirichlet and Neumann Laplacians. Outside the critical regime, one recovers the corresponding Dirichlet or Neumann asymptotics. We also establish a Pólya-type inequality for the counting function whenever the ratio of the Robin parameter and $\sqrtλ$ exceeds a dimension-dependent threshold.

</details>

### [2] [A Hilbert Module Approach to Classical and Quantum Reference Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01894)
*Lucus Brady*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文利用希尔伯特模和C*-动力系统的等变对应关系，为C*-代数建立了经典和量子参考系的模型，展示了经典模型作为量子模型的交换基线，并讨论了量子模型如何编码量子参考系的希尔伯特空间操作公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子参考系是量子力学和量子引力中的基础概念，但缺乏统一的代数框架。作者旨在利用C*-代数这一数学工具，为经典和量子参考系提供一个统一的、严格的数学建模框架。

Method: 采用C*-代数框架，借助希尔伯特模和C*-动力系统的等变对应关系来构造经典与量子参考系模型。通过比较两种模型的结构，揭示经典模型（交换情形）作为量子模型的基线，并展示量子模型能涵盖已知的希尔伯特空间操作公式。

Result: 成功构建了经典与量子参考系的C*-代数模型，证明了经典模型提供交换基线，并论证量子模型包含了希尔伯特空间操作公式中量子参考系的编码。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子参考系提供了严格的代数基础，统一了经典与量子参考系的描述，并附带具体例子展示其适用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a model for classical and quantum reference frames for $C^*$-algebras by utilizing Hilbert modules and equivariant correspondences of $C^*$-dynamical systems. We show the classical model provides a commutative baseline for the quantum model, and we discuss how the quantum model encodes the Hilbert space operational formulation of quantum reference frames. We also include various explicit examples for both classical and quantum reference frames.

</details>


### [3] [Disjoint strong transitive operators on Hilbert C*-modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02281)
*Song-Ung Ri,Hyon-Hui Ju*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hilbert C*-模上的不相交F-传递算子，其中F是N的子集的有限不变Furstenberg族。作者刻画了标准Hilbert模上广义双边加权移位算子的不相交F-传递性，给出其不相交混沌的充分条件及具体例子，并刻画了非单位C*-代数上广义平移算子的不相交F-传递性及其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不相交F-传递算子这一泛函分析与遍历理论交叉领域的核心概念，特别是将其推广到Hilbert C*-模这一更一般的算子理论框架中，以深化对算子动力学的理解。

Method: 利用Furstenberg族的有限不变性作为工具，对广义双边加权移位算子和广义平移算子这两类典型算子进行F-传递性的不相交性刻画，并通过具体例子验证所得结论。

Result: 给出了标准Hilbert模上广义双边加权移位算子不相交F-传递的完整刻画，提供了其不相交混沌的充分条件并构造了具体实例；同时完成了非单位C*-代数上广义平移算子不相交F-传递性的刻画。

Conclusion: 本文在Hilbert C*-模框架下系统地建立了不相交F-传递性理论，为广义加权移位算子和广义平移算子提供了新的动力学分类结果，拓展了经典超循环理论在算子和C*-代数情境下的适用范围。

Abstract: This paper is about disjoint F-transitive operators on Hilbert C*-modules, where F is a finitely invariant Furstenberg family of subsets of N. We characterize disjoint F-transitivity for generalized bilateral weighted shift operators on the standard Hilbert module over C*-algebra of compact operators on the separable Hilbert space. Then we give a sufficient condition for these operators to be disjoint chaotic and give concrete examples. We also characterize disjoint F-transitivity for generalized translation operators on the non-unitary C*-algebra and provide some applications.

</details>


### [4] [Vector valued continuous function spaces as $C^\ast$-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02680)
*Neha Hotwani,T. S. S. R. K. Rao*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究了向量值C*-代数C(Ω, A)的单位球中C*-极值点与线性极值点结构的比较。作者证明了若A具有忠实的迹态，则C(Ω, A)1中的任何C*-极值点都是酉元；同时确定了C*-极值点与逐点C*-极值点重合的C*-代数类别——即具有可分预对偶且满足Radon-Nikodým性质的von Neumann代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续先前工作[HR]，进一步探究C*-极值点与单位球的线性极值结构之间的关系，旨在扩大使得"C*-极值点即为极值点"这一结论成立的C*-代数类别，并界定C*-极值点与逐点C*-极值点等价的条件。

Method: 通过引入忠实迹态条件来构造证明，利用von Neumann代数的可分预对偶及Radon-Nikodým性质来建立C*-极值点与逐点C*-极值点的一致性结论。

Result: (1) 若C*-代数A具有忠实迹态，则C(Ω, A)单位球中的任意C*-极值点必为酉元；(2) 对于具有可分预对偶并满足Radon-Nikodým性质的von Neumann代数，C(Ω, A)1中C*-极值点与逐点C*-极值点的概念一致。

Conclusion: 该研究进一步拓展了C*-极值点与线性极值点比较的理论框架，为后续研究提供了更广泛的C*-代数类别适用条件。

Abstract: Let \(Ω\) be a compact Hausdorff space, and let \(\cA\) be a unital \(C^*\)-algebra. In this study, we continue our examination of the comparison between \(C^*\)-extreme points and linear extremal structures of the unit ball, in the vector-valued \(C^*\)-algebra \(C(Ω, \cA)\), building upon the work initiated in \cite{HR}. We first enlarge the class of $C^\ast$-algebras in which a $ C^\ast$-extreme point is an extreme point. We demonstrate that if \(\cA\) has a faithful tracial state, then any \(C^*\)-extreme point of the unit ball \(C(Ω, \cA)_1\) is a unitary. Additionally, we identify a classes of \(C^*\)-algebras where the concepts of \(C^*\)-extreme and pointwise \(C^*\)-extreme points in \(C(Ω, \cA)_1\) coincide. We show this holds if a von Neumann algebra has a separable predual with the Radon-Nikodým property.

</details>


### [5] [Noncommutative majorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02799)
*Matthew Kennedy,Paul Skoufranis*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非交换优序（noncommutative majorization）理论，将Hardy、Littlewood和Pólya的经典优序理论推广到不一定交换的对称矩阵元组上。利用Davidson和第一作者提出的非交换凸性理论与非交换Choquet理论，定义并刻画了推广经典优序的非交换优序关系。作为应用，得到了在两个有限矩阵集之间存在保迹完全正映射（即量子信道）的新的充要条件，并给出反例说明该映射不能总是选为混合酉映射（即使在局部情况下）。文章还讨论了验证非交换优序的计算困难性，并将结果推广到非迹情形，刻画了保持任意忠实态或酉完全正映射的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典优序理论在矩阵与量子信息领域有广泛应用，但传统优序仅适用于可交换（可对易）的矩阵元组。在量子信息中，非交换矩阵元组之间的转换条件（如量子信道的存在性）尚未有系统的优序理论框架。作者旨在建立一种不依赖交换性的优序理论，为量子信道插值等核心问题提供新的刻画工具。

Method: 以凸非交换函数定义非交换优序关系；利用非交换凸性理论和非交换Choquet理论（由Davidson和第一作者近期引入）来对非交换优序进行表征。基于该序刻画，推导保迹完全正映射（量子信道）插值两个有限矩阵集的充要条件，并进一步推广到保持任意忠实态或酉完全正映射的更一般情形。通过具体反例论证混合酉映射的局限性，并讨论该序的计算验证复杂度。

Result: 建立了非交换优序的完整理论刻画；给出了量子信道插值问题的新的充要条件；构造了反例表明在某些情况下即使局部也存在无法选择混合酉映射的情形；证明了结果可推广到非迹情形，得到保持任意忠实态的完全正映射存在的更一般表征。同时指出验证非交换优序存在计算困难性。

Conclusion: 非交换优序理论为经典优序在非交换领域的系统推广提供了一个统一框架，不仅在量子信道存在性问题上给出了新的充要条件，而且揭示了与混合酉映射的本质差异。该理论为进一步研究非交换矩阵间的变换结构及量子信息处理中的序关系奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce a theory of noncommutative majorization that extends the classical majorization theory introduced by Hardy, Littlewood and Pólya to tuples of self-adjoint matrices that do not necessarily commute. We define and characterize a noncommutative majorization order that extends the classical majorization order. The definition is in terms of convex noncommutative functions, and we utilize the noncommutative convexity theory and noncommutative Choquet theory recently introduced by Davidson and the first author. As an application, we obtain a new necessary and sufficient condition for the existence of a trace-preserving completely positive map, i.e. a quantum channel, that interpolates between two finite sets of matrices. We give examples demonstrating that it is not always possible for this map to be chosen mixed unitary, even locally. We also address the computational difficulty of verifying the noncommutative majorization order. Our results further apply beyond the tracial case, and we obtain more general results characterizing the existence of unital completely positive maps that preserve an arbitrary faithful state or even unital completely positive map.

</details>


### [6] [Actions of quantum groups on dual operator spaces and their crossed products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02822)
*Jason Crann,Joeri De Ro,Jacek Krajczok*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究了局部紧量子群作用下的对偶算子空间范畴，证明了Fubini交叉积函子与弱*交叉积函子相等当且仅当该量子群具有Haagerup-Kraus逼近性质。通过一个自然同构，进一步给出了该逼近性质的L^1(𝔾)-模刻画，并探讨了Fubini交叉积函子的正合性与量子群的可amenability性质之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在算子代数与量子群理论中，交叉积构造是对偶理论的核心工具。此前关于Fubini交叉积与弱*交叉积相等的条件仅在经典群情形下已明确，将其推广到局部紧量子群并联系于Haagerup-Kraus逼近性质，是自然且重要的理论延伸。

Method: 本文方法上采用函子论视角，系统研究对偶算子空间范畴上两个交叉积函子的等价性；利用Fubini交叉积函子与某个L^1(𝔾)-模CB函子的自然同构作为桥梁，将量子群的逼近性质转化为具体的模逼近性质问题；最后通过分析Fubini交叉积函子的正合性来刻画量子群的amenability性质。

Result: 得出Fubini交叉积函子与弱*交叉积函子相等的充要条件是𝔾具有Haagerup-Kraus逼近性质；基于自然同构获得了该逼近性质的L^1(𝔾)-模逼近等价刻画；此外将Fubini交叉积函子的正合性与𝔾的amenability性质建立了关联。

Conclusion: 本文从函子论角度统一并推广了经典群论中的相关结果，为局部紧量子群的逼近性质与交叉积构造之间的关系提供了新的刻画框架，其结论对算子空间理论及量子群调和分析具有推动意义。

Abstract: We study the category of dual operator spaces equipped with an action of a locally compact quantum group $\mathbb{G}$. The Fubini crossed product functor $-\rtimes^\mathcal{F} \mathbb{G}$ and the weak$^*$-crossed product functor $-\bar{\rtimes}\mathbb{G}$ are shown to be equal if and only if $\mathbb{G}$ has the approximation property of Haagerup and Kraus. Using the natural isomorphism $-\rtimes^\mathcal{F}\mathbb{G}\cong {}_{L^1(\mathbb{G})}\mathcal{CB}(B(L^2(\mathbb{G}))_*, -)$, this leads to a characterization of the approximation property of $\mathbb{G}$ via an $L^1(\mathbb{G})$-module approximation property for $B(L^2(\mathbb{G}))_*$. Finally, exactness of the Fubini crossed product functor is investigated and related to amenability properties of $\mathbb{G}$.

</details>

### [7] [On Exposed (Symmetric) Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01835)
*Sheldon Dantas,Martin Mazzitelli,Jorge Tomás Rodríguez*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个射影张量积的例子，其单位球包含一个"暴露张量"（exposed tensor）但该张量不是"初等张量"（elementary tensor），并将该构造推广到对称射影张量积以及任意多个因子的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在射影张量积的理论中，研究单位球的极端点和暴露点是理解其几何结构的重要途径。先前已知极端张量与初等张量之间存在联系，但在"暴露"这个更强的概念下，初等性与暴露性之间的关系尚不清楚。本文旨在构造反例，说明在射影张量积中暴露张量并不一定等价于初等张量。

Method: 首先构造一个具体的射影张量积示例，在其中找到一个属于单位球边界上的暴露点但不是初等张量的元素。接着将该构造转移到对称射影张量积框架中，通过适当的对称化处理得到类似结果。最后，将两个构造分别推广到具有任意有限多个因子的射影张量积以及任意次对称射影张量积。

Result: 成功构造出射影张量积单位球中的暴露但非初等的张量示例，并在对称射影张量积中获得了对偶结果，且两种构造均可推广到任意多个因子和任意次对称张量积。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，射影张量积及对称射影张量积单位球的暴露点集合严格大于其初等张量集合，揭示了射影张量积几何结构中暴露性与初等性之间并不等价的重要性质。

Abstract: We provide an example of a projective tensor product whose unit ball contains an exposed tensor which is not elementary. We then transfer the construction to the symmetric projective tensor product and obtain an analogous result in that setting. Finally, we extend both constructions to projective tensor products with an arbitrary number of factors and to symmetric projective tensor products of every degree.

</details>


### [8] [The Order of the Non-universal Tree of a Hilbert Space with Respect to the Haar Basis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01885)
*Sam Whitmire*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于任何可分Hilbert空间H，其非通用树T_{NU}(H)相对于C(2^N)的Haar基对应的序数o(T_{NU}(H)) = ω + 1，表明在非通用树这一序数度量下，Hilbert空间类是复杂度最低的可分Banach空间类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bossard于1994年引入非通用树T_{NU}(X)以对不含C(2^N)同构副本的可分Banach空间X进行结构刻画，结合良基树的序运算可对可分Banach空间的有限维子空间同构复杂度进行分类。然而多年以来，尚无人直接展示具体空间和基下的非通用树的阶，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 作者选取可分Hilbert空间H这一具体空间，并相对于C(2^N)的Haar基来考察其非通用树T_{NU}(H)的阶o(T_{NU}(H))。通过精确分析哈aar基下有限维子空间同构关系在该序结构中的分布与高度，直接计算出该序数为ω+1。

Result: 证明了若H是任意可分Hilbert空间，相对于C(2^N)的Haar基有o(T_{NU}(H)) = ω + 1。此结果表明在此度量下Hilbert空间类是最低复杂度类。

Conclusion: 该结果首次给出了非通用树之阶的具体实例计算，确立了Hilbert空间在基于非通用树序数的Banach空间分类框架中处于最简位置，为该领域的进一步具体化研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: In his 1994 doctoral thesis, Bossard introduced the notion of the non-universal tree $T_{NU}(X)$ associated with each separable Banach space $X$ which does not contain an isomorphic copy of $C(2^\mathbb{N})$. Together with the order operation defined on well-founded trees, we obtain a method of classifying the complexity of separable Banach spaces by the degree of isomorphism of finite-dimensional subspaces of $C(2^\mathbb{N})$. Despite further refinement of this concept in later years, we are unaware of any specific instances of direct exhibitions of the order of the non-universal tree for a concrete space and basis. We show that if $H$ is any separable Hilbert space, then $o(T_{NU}(H)) = ω + 1$ when taken with respect to the Haar basis for $C(2^\mathbb{N})$, demonstrating that the class of Hilbert spaces is the least complex class with respect to this measurement when considering this basis.

</details>


### [9] [On Sum Ranges for $3n$-convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01926)
*Preston Martens*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Riemann重排定理(RRT)在弱收敛形式（如2n-收敛和3n-收敛）下的推广。研究表明，对于3n-收敛，其和值域在某些条件下仍为加法子群，而在另一些条件下则不能成为子群，从而证明了3n-收敛比2n-收敛具有更丰富的结构深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典RRT表明无穷级数的重排可改变收敛值，但关于可交换性的研究主要限于标准收敛。作者希望将级数重排的概念推广到更弱的收敛形式（如每第2或第3个索引部分和收敛），并考察这些情形下的和值域结构，特别是研究3n-收敛是否像2n-收敛那样能产生更丰富的代数结构。

Method: 作者在2n-收敛已知结果（和值域可产生平移加法子群）的基础上，系统分析3n-收敛的和值域。具体方法包括构造特定条件，证明在这些条件下3n-和值域仍为加法子群，并构造反例情形证明3n-和值域不能为子群，从而展示3n-收敛与2n-收敛的结构差异。

We show, among other results, that every $\widehat f$ admits a non-trivial invariant subspace whenever the underlying metric space contains a compact ball, or has at least two connected components one of which has non-empty interior. Abstract: We study the $\mathcal F$-transitivity and topological $\mathcal F$-recurrence of left translation semigroups on weighted $L^p$-spaces over directed metric trees. Motivated by the recent work of Mangino and Vargas-Moreno on hypercyclicity and weak mixing for these semigroups, we investigate the different problem of $\mathcal F$-transitivity, where the entire return-time set is required to belong to a prescribed finitely invariant Furstenberg family. Assuming that the weight is $p$-admissible, we obtain necessary and sufficient integral conditions for both rooted and rootless trees, and establish the equivalence between $\mathcal F$-transitivity and topological $\mathcal F$-recurrence. In the rooted case, the criteria depend only on the weights along descendant edges. In the rootless case, an additional ancestor term and a measurable cancellation construction reflect the backward geometry of the tree. Our approach is based on measurable weighted minimization and quantitative estimates over subsets of the edge parameter interval. Examples on homogeneous rooted trees and rootless trees with a free left end show that branching may determine the dynamics, but forward branching alone does not guarantee $\mathcal F$-transitivity in the rootless setting.

Result: 本文成功证明了Lyubarskii和Nes关于第一Hermite函数帧集的猜想。具体结果是：任何使Gabor系统不成框架的亚临界格子乘积δ=ab都必须具有(q-1)/q的形式，其中q≥2为整数。该结果为第一个Hermite函数的帧集提供了精确完整的刻画。

Conclusion: 通过代数与解析方法的巧妙结合（Wronskian放大技术与高斯零点密度定理），本文完全解决了第一Hermite函数帧集的刻画问题。这一结果不仅验证了先前猜想，也为Gabor框架理论中其他高斯型或Hermite型窗函数的帧集研究提供了重要的方法论借鉴。

Abstract: We determine the frame set of the first Hermite function, proving a conjecture of Lyubarskii and Nes. By taking the Wronskian of $N$ translates of $F$, we amplify its critical points to zeros of multiplicity at least $N-1$. After rescaling, this Wronskian is again a Gaussian shift-invariant function. A Gaussian zero-density theorem then gives $(N-1)/(Nδ)\leq1$. Varying $N$ over the range allowed by the construction forces every subcritical lattice product $δ=ab$ for which the system is not a frame to equal $(q-1)/q$ for some integer $q\geq2$.

</details>

Abstract: In homage to Hofstadter, we call a compact, citable specification of definitions, theorem, proof mechanism, and examples a mathematical koan when a full paper can be reconstructed and verified from it, including in reader-specific forms generated by AI. Every AI-solvable problem is itself a koan, although reconstruction need not be cheap; the present article is an expansion of one.
  We introduce Cartan convexity for self-adjoint free functions: locally, such a function agrees with a locally bounded matrix-convex free function. If the variable set has cardinality $τ$ and $λ=\max\{τ,\aleph_0\}$, every bounded real free set has a universal direct sum on a Hilbert space of dimension at most $2^λ$; every point of the set is a reducing summand of an amplification of this sum. Applying the noncommutative Kraus--butterfly theorem at that universal point yields an extension to an open matrix-convex neighborhood of the noncommutative convex hull. For normal affine pencils, the extension domain also contains the bounded strong closure of the hull.
  We prove an analogous theorem for a graph embedding $Γ$. A local convex lift through $Γ$ extends to a neighborhood of $Γ^{-1}(\operatorname{co}_{\mathrm{nc}}Γ(K))$ and admits a butterfly realization in the $Γ$-coordinates. We distinguish this lift condition from intrinsic $Γ$-convexity. For $Γ(x,y)=(x,y,y^2)$, the intrinsically $Γ$-affine polynomial $xy+yx$ has no convex lift germ at the origin, whereas a single quadratic coordinate suffices to lift every uniformly real analytic germ.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: This survey paper provides a comprehensive study of unitarily invariant norms on the algebra of $n \times n$ matrices. This investigation leads naturally to the theory of symmetric gauge functions, a class of norms on $\mathbb{R}^n$ characterized by invariance and monotonicity properties. We develop the necessary framework by examining absolute and monotone norms and establishing their equivalence, thereby offering additional insight into the classical Hardy-Littlewood-Pólya theorem on majorization. The theory of majorization is further explored through its connections with doubly stochastic matrices, convexity, and fundamental results such as the Birkhoff and Radó theorems, as well as König's theorem on term rank and line rank. We also study weak majorization and derive a characterization that plays a crucial role in proving the monotonicity of symmetric gauge functions. On the spectral side, we review key results in matrix analysis, including the Courant-Fischer min-max theorem, the Cauchy interlacing theorem, and Ky Fan's majorization theorem, along with a weak subadditivity result for singular values of arbitrary matrices. These ingredients culminate in a detailed proof of von Neumann's characterization of unitarily invariant norms, which provides a complete and elegant description of this class of norms. Some illustrative examples, as well as the Ky Fan domination principle as an application, are also presented.

</details>

For complete open simply connected LCF manifolds of nonnegative scalar curvature, we obtain Euclidean rigidity under additional topological hypotheses at infinity (and, in dimension three, from vanishing second homology alone). We also show that, in dimensions $n\ge4$, the Euclidean conclusion can fail when neither of the two additional topological hypotheses is assumed: we construct complete contractible examples of PSC that are not homeomorphic to $\mathbb{R}^n$.

As the scale shrinks, this oscillation repeatedly changes the sign of the closing error, producing infinitely many parameter values for which the metric closes. The closing scales satisfy an asymptotic logarithmic quantization law, with successive ratio tending to \(e^{-2π/\sqrt{15}}\). We also determine the geometry of the resulting sequence. Abstract: The Fibonacci and Lucas sequences are old friends in recreational number theory. Here we ask a simple question: which of their terms $F_n$ and $L_n$ are Smith numbers? A Smith number is a composite integer whose decimal digit sum equals the sum of the decimal digits of its prime factors, counted with multiplicity. The question is easy to state, but for large $n$ it quickly becomes a factorization problem. Using available complete factorization data, we carried out computational searches in both sequences and obtained new Smith terms in each of them. For the Lucas sequence, the first author's search found ten Smith terms with indices below 1000: 3, 95, 105, 114, 183, 437, 609, 682, 827, 902. These results were published in the On-Line Encyclopedia of Integer Sequences as OEIS A395686. Four further indices, 1090, 1153, 1215, 1378, were subsequently added to the sequence by Sean A. Irvine. For the Fibonacci sequence, starting from the cases already recorded in OEIS A382922, our search produced seven further Smith numbers: $F_{1440}$, $F_{1554}$, $F_{1596}$, $F_{1863}$, $F_{2256}$, $F_{2277}$, $F_{2559}$. For the even-index cases, the identity $F_{2n}=F_nL_n$ allows available Fibonacci and Lucas factorization data to be combined to recover the complete factorizations needed for the Smith test. Surprisingly, the two searches also meet at index 827: both $F_{827}$ and $L_{827}$ are Smith numbers.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Consider the polynomial $f=x^N+y^N-z^N$ where $x,\,y$ and $z$ are positive integers and $N \ge 3$ is an integer. By Fermat's Last Theorem, $f$ is never zero so that its reciprocal, $1/f$, has no singularities. We therefore study the finite sum of the reciprocal: $S(m,N)=\sum_{x=1}^m\sum_{y=1}^m\sum_{z=1}^{m}\frac{1}{f}$. The terms $1/f$ can be positive, negative and their magnitude is less than unity. A key observation is that $S(m,N)$ can be split into two convenient parts: a dominant contribution $D(m,N)$ that has a simple analytical expression and a remainder $R(m,N)$ which is more complicated but negligible compared to $D(m,N)$. Therefore, $S(m,N)$ is almost identical to $D(m,N)$. The analytical expression for $D(m,N)$ is $(2\,m-1)\,H_m^{(N)}$ where $H_m^{(N)}=\sum_{x=1}^m\frac{1}{x^N}$ approaches quickly the Riemann zeta function $ζ(N)$ as $m$ increases. Therefore, the original sum $S(m,N)$ has a simple expression: it is basically linear in $m$ with slope equal to $2\,ζ(N)$. Its linear behavior is not an asymptotic result; plots of $S(m,N)$ vs. $m$ for different $N$ show a straight line starting at $m=1$. $S(m,N)$ deviates slightly from a straight line over a small interval $8\le m\le 12$ for the case $N=3$. This slight deviation is due to Fermat near misses where $x^3+y^3-z^3=\pm 1$ (for $z\ne x$ and $z\ne y$); these create a jump in the remainder $R(m,3)$ at $m=9$. We make a numerical and analytical study of the remainder $R(m,N)$. From the numerical analysis, $R(m,N)$ converges for $N\ge 4$ but it was harder to tell whether $N=3$ converged. An analytical study based on a comparison of $R(m,N)$ to its Cauchy principal value integral, shows that $R(m,3)$ likely diverges logarithmically. It also shows that $R(m,N)$ converges for $N\ge 4$ in agreement with the numerical analysis. We discuss in the conclusion some interesting questions for future investigation.

</details>

Abstract: We obtain a new, conceptually simpler, unconditional proof that more than $67.25\%$ of the non-trivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function are simple and on the critical line, and that at least $83.62\%$ of the non-trivial zeros are distinct. The list also includes four families that are eigenfunctions of a fourth-order differential operator, whereas no second-order operator is available. Moreover, orthogonal polynomials on the wrapped quadratic surfaces that are eigenvalues of a second-order differential operator on the surface are also studied.

Method: 文章发展了两条独立的技术路线。其一为初等ODE方法：通过构造合适的微分方程（源于函数的代数或微分结构），利用Sturm型零点计数技巧在一般情形下获得多项式界；此方法同样适用于控制一维高斯混合的临界点数。其二为PDE方法，适用于正二次多项式平方根的情形：将问题转化为穿孔双曲平面上Helmholtz方程Δu+λu=0解的节点域计数问题，从而借助节点域定理等工具获得线性界。后一方法的副产品建立了R^3\{(x,0,0)}上轴对称调和函数与双曲平面上特征值1/4的Laplace-Beltrami本征函数之间的对应关系。

Result: 1) 一般函数族在区间I上的零点数获得关于n为多项式、关于d为线性、与指数α_k无关的上界，优于已有指数界。2) 对正二次多项式平方根的实线性组合，在R上证明零点数≤2n，回答了Alon的问题。3) 修正论证后得到与Maxwell猜想相关的Gabrielov-Novikov-Shapiro问题的线性界。4) ODE方法还导出一维高斯混合临界点数的多项式界。

Conclusion: 本文显著改进了多项式幂线性组合零点计数的上界，为核心问题提供了线性界的答案，并揭示了该问题与双曲平面PDE节点域计数及调和函数对称结构之间的深层联系，为后续在指数任意实数情形下进一步优化界和探索相关几何-谱联系奠定了方法与概念基础。

Abstract: We obtain upper bounds for the number of real zeros of functions of the form $$ f(x) = \sum_{k=1}^{n} c_k \bigl(P_k(x)\bigr)^{α_k}, $$ where $c_k, α_k \in \mathbb{R}$ and each $P_k$ is a real polynomial of degree at most $d$ that is non-negative on an interval $I\subset \mathbb{R}$. We improve previously known exponential upper bounds for the number of roots on $I$ to bounds that are polynomial in $n$, linear in $d$, and independent of the exponents $α_k$.
  For linear combinations of square roots of positive quadratic polynomials on $\mathbb{R}$ we prove the linear bound $2n$, answering a question of N.~Alon. A modification of the argument yields a linear bound for a question of A.~Gabrielov, D.~Novikov, and B.~Shapiro related to Maxwell's conjecture.
  The article describes two independent approaches: an elementary ODE method in the general case, which also gives a polynomial bound for the number of critical points of one dimensional Gaussian mixtures, and a PDE method for the case of positive quadratic polynomials, which connects the problem to the number of nodal domains of solutions to $Δu + λu = 0$ on the punctured hyperbolic plane. As a byproduct of the second approach, we describe a curious relation between axially symmetric harmonic functions on $\mathbb{R}^3\setminus\{(x,0,0)\}$ and Laplace-Beltrami eigenfunctions on the hyperbolic plane with eigenvalue $1/4$.

</details>

Conclusion: McKean提出的熵产生单调性问题在常数角截面、γ∈[0,1]的空间齐次Boltzmann方程框架内获得了否定答案，表明Boltzmann方程向平衡态逼近过程中的熵产生行为可能比经典推测更为复杂。

Abstract: In 1966, McKean asked whether the entropy production of the Boltzmann equation must be monotone decreasing in time. We show that this is not the case even in the space-homogeneous setting for the Boltzmann collision operator with a constant angular cross section and the kinetic parameter $γ\in [0,1]$. This recovers the classical case of hard spheres and the simplest case of Maxwell molecules. Our examples are radially symmetric mixtures of Maxwellians.

Abstract: We study the hypoellipticity of operators on a product type Lorentzian manifold where the time variable is periodic. In particular, we prove that hypoellipticity holds for such time-periodic equations, with a stronger estimate when the mass parameter or time period lies outside a set of arbitrarily small measure. We consider both compact and noncompact spatial manifolds and provide explicit examples involving the wave operator. The proof relies on a Fourier series decomposition and asymptotics for eigenvalue counting functions.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆散射问题旨在从散射数据恢复势函数或度量结构。在固定能量下，经典的逆散射问题通常需要全场散射信息；本文探索在何种条件下，仅需半经典极限下的高阶散射信息（或固定能量的微分截面）即可实现势的唯一重建，从而将半经典方法与经典刚性结果结合以拓展逆散射理论的适用范围。

Method: (1) 利用Ingremeau对散射矩阵作用在相干态上的描述，建立半经典散射矩阵在h→0时的行为与经典散射映射之间的联系；(2) 结合Stefanov-Uhlmann-Vasy的经典刚性定理，在势能低于能量的virial条件下获得势的唯一性；(3) 对径向排斥势，在径向力单调性假设下证明经典散射关系为单分支，进而结合Robert-Tamura的半经典散射渐近和经典Firsov-Abel反演公式重构势；(4) 将类似框架推广至紧支撑度量扰动情形。

Result: 证明了：若紧支撑势对应的半经典散射矩阵之差在算子范数意义下为o(1)（h→0），则在非俘获能量下两者的经典散射映射一致；在自然virial条件且能量高于势时，此数据足以唯一确定势函数。对满足单调性条件的径向短程排斥势，固定能量的微分截面可唯一确定经典可达区域的势。对满足简单条件的紧支撑度量扰动同样建立了刚性结果。

Conclusion: 本文通过弥合半经典散射与经典逆散射之间的鸿沟，获得了从固定能量散射数据中恢复势函数和度量扰动的若干唯一性/刚性定理，展示了半经典方法（相干态分析、WKB渐近）与经典方法（反演公式、刚性定理）结合的优势。其结果对逆散射理论中固定能量数据的信息含量问题给出了新的明确定义。

Abstract: We investigate inverse scattering at a fixed energy for semiclassical
  Schrödinger operators with smooth potentials. For compactly supported
  potentials, we show that, at a non-trapping energy, if the corresponding
  semiclassical scattering matrices differ by $o(1)$ in operator norm as
  $h\to0$, then the associated classical scattering maps coincide. The
  proof relies on Ingremeau's description of the action of the scattering
  matrix on coherent states. Combined with a classical rigidity result of
  Stefanov--Uhlmann--Vasy, this yields uniqueness of the potential under a
  natural virial condition, provided the energy lies above the potential.
  We then consider radial short-range repulsive potentials. Under a
  monotonicity assumption on the radial force, the classical scattering
  relation has a single branch. Combining the semiclassical scattering
  asymptotics of Robert--Tamura with the classical Firsov--Abel inversion
  formula, we show that the differential cross section at a single fixed
  energy determines the potential throughout the classically accessible
  region. Finally, we obtain an analogous rigidity result for simple
  compactly supported metric perturbations.

</details>

Result: 成功构造了算子 (∂t−iΔx)^s 的半群定义和等价的局部扩展问题，显式给出振荡泊松核，建立了历史数据能量之间的精确恒等式，并在扩展诱导的温和意义下获得非线性记忆问题的适定性。

Conclusion: 该工作为时空双重非局部分数阶薛定谔算子建立了完整的分析框架，为带记忆的非线性薛定谔方程提供了严格的适定性理论，为后续研究该类非局部记忆问题提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: We study nonlinear Schrödinger equations with memory through an extension theory for the fully fractional Schrödinger operator \[ (\partial_t-iΔ_x)^s,\qquad 0<s<1. \] Because the operator is nonlocal in both space and time, its natural Cauchy problem is posed with a prescribed past history rather than a classical initial condition. We construct a semigroup definition of $(\partial_t-iΔ_x)^s$, derive an equivalent local extension problem in one additional spatial variable, and compute the corresponding oscillatory Poisson kernel explicitly. The extension yields a Poisson lifting of history data and an intrinsic quadratic form on histories, establishing an explicit identity between the weighted bulk energy of the lifting and a nonlocal energy determined entirely by the prescribed past evolution. As an application, and in combination with the nonlinear boundary-interaction theory developed in our companion paper, we obtain a well-posedness theory for the associated nonlinear memory problem. The well-posedness is established in an extension-induced mild sense: for a general history in the natural energy space the future evolution is defined as the boundary trace of the corresponding solution in the half-space, and the fractional equation itself is recovered, in $L^2$ and for positive times, under an additional graph-domain hypothesis.

Motivation: 长期以来，Pólya–Szegő猜想断言等面积n边形中正则n边形最小化第一个Laplace–Dirichlet特征值，其Neumann类比（正则多边形最大化第一个非零Neumann特征值）同样是一个长期悬而未决的问题。该论文旨在解决这一Neumann类比中最基本的四边形情形，填补该方向上的空白。

Result: 成功证明了等面积凸平面四边形中正方形唯一最大化第一个非零Laplace–Neumann特征值，给出了该问题的完整证明。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了Neumann型Pólya–Szegő猜想在四边形情形下的成立，其证明框架（Rayleigh–Ritz技术与严格认证方法结合）为推广到一般n边形情形提供了可借鉴的途径。

Abstract: We prove that among convex planar quadrilaterals of equal area, the square uniquely maximizes the first nonzero Laplace--Neumann eigenvalue. This is the quadrilateral case of the Neumann analogue of the long-standing Pólya--Szegő conjecture, which asserts that the regular $n$-gon minimizes the first Laplace--Dirichlet eigenvalue among $n$-gons of equal area. The proof uses a Rayleigh--Ritz lower bound, obtained from the span of the first five nonconstant Neumann modes of the square, on a smooth auxiliary functional whose minimization implies the original maximization. Local maximality at the square follows from a symmetry-reduced Hessian, computed in closed form, together with a certified second-order difference-quotient test that establishes the local inequality on an explicit ball around the square. Global maximality is then established by a certified box covering of the parameter space, certifying on each box a direct upper bound on the first Rayleigh--Ritz eigenvalue. Both certifications operate entirely on integrals over a fixed reference square and avoid a posteriori finite-element bounds on the perturbed quadrilateral.

</details>

Abstract: In this manuscript, we determine the critical exponent for the two-dimensional semilinear wave equation with strong damping. A recent result in D'Abbicco (arXiv, 2026) gives global in-time small data solutions for $p>\frac{10}{3}$, whereas we in the paper prove finite-time blow-up for $2<p\leqslant\frac{10}{3}$ under an averaged sign condition on the initial velocity. Together with the previously known blow-up result for $1<p\leqslant3$, the threshold for the power nonlinearity $|u|^p$ is \begin{align*}
  p=p_{\mathrm{crit}}=\frac{10}{3}. \end{align*} We prove positivity of the full two-dimensional velocity fundamental solution and construct a positive averaged kernel, which allows us to establish a nonlinear lower-bound iteration in a parabolic region.

</details>

Motivation: Córdoba–Córdoba–Fontelos方程是研究非局部界面运动中有限时间奇性形成的重要模型。引入乘性随机噪声（尤其是近乎斜自伴的伪微分算子噪声，包含经典输运噪声）具有物理相关性，但其对爆破机制的影响尚未被系统研究。本文旨在回答随机输运背景下CCF方程是否仍保持确定情形下的有限时间爆破特性，并刻画其精确的爆破速率。

Conclusion: 本文表明，在几乎斜自伴的乘性Stratonovich噪声（特别是输运噪声）扰动下，CCF方程的有限时间奇性形成机制对随机扰动具有鲁棒性：奇性仍以Type-I型方式形成，且爆破速率由终态非局部能量的Cesàro平均极限所精确刻画。这为随机非局部输运方程中奇性动力学研究提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We study the stochastic Córdoba--Córdoba--Fontelos equation driven by multiplicative Stratonovich noise. The noise amplitude is allowed to be a pseudo-differential operator whose leading part is nearly skew-adjoint. This class contains classical transport noise and also permits genuinely nonlocal perturbations. We first develop a local-in-time theory for maximal classical solutions in Sobolev spaces, proving existence, uniqueness, and a blow-up criterion. We then consider the special case of Stratonovich transport. For sufficiently large initial nonlocal steepness at a global maximum, we prove finite-time blow-up with arbitrarily high prescribed probability and obtain an explicit upper bound on the lifespan. Finally, on the event that the nonlocal steepness at the transported maximum diverges, we establish a conditional Type-I upper bound. When the terminal Cesàro average of the normalised nonlocal energy converges, we further identify the exact leading-order blow-up rate in terms of its limiting value.

Utilizing an asymptotic slope envelope, we prove that no non-negative linear-growth supersolution can exist beyond $T_{\mathrm{crit}}$. When $q(p-1)< 1$, we prove this property assuming $V(x_0, r)\leq Cr^{N}$ and $p>N[1-q(p-1)]$, which matches the threshold in $\mathbb{R}^{n}$ with $N=n$. We also show that solutions on the hyperbolic space $\mathbb{H}^{n}$ have a finite extinction time in the case $q(p-1)< 1$, which implies that the conservation of mass property does not hold. Using the conservation of mass result in the case $q(p-1)=1$, we also prove a $L^{p-1}$- Liouville property, which partially answers a conjecture stated by I. Holopainen \cite{holopainen2000sharp}.

Abstract: We consider generalized principal eigenvalues of one-dimensional nonlocal dispersal operators with a drift of fixed sign. A principal difficulty in this non-self-adjoint setting is the absence of a variational characterization of the principal value. In the symmetric drift-free problem, the critical nonlocal-to-local limit can be treated through a quadratic variational structure and Sobolev-seminorm approximation as Berestycki-Coville-Vo \cite{BCV}. The drift destroys this structure and, on a bounded interval, introduces at the same time a one-sided inflow--outflow boundary geometry. Replacing the missing variational argument by estimates which remain stable under singular rescaling is therefore a central technical issue. Our approach is direct : we work with a single generalized principal value and prove the maximum principle, simplicity in the positive cone and the scaling limits from the equation itself, without passing through auxiliary generalized principal eigenvalues analogous to $λ_1'$ and $λ_1''$ in Berestycki--Rossi \cite{BR}. The replacement mechanisms are directional Harnack inequalities and coefficient barriers, direct--adjoint identities, logarithmic Collatz--Wielandt transforms, Fourier coercivity of zero extensions and scale-dependent localization. At the critical diffusive scale, Fourier compactness and an exact energy--transport identity recover the missing inflow Dirichlet condition and identify the local Dirichlet limit without a Rayleigh quotient. For variable coefficients we determine the complete small-range phase diagram on bounded intervals and on the line, and we obtain sharp large-range three-term asymptotics by a rank-one reduction and a corner Laplace analysis in the advective travel-time variable. In the homogeneous whole-line problem the critical correction is of order $σ^2$.

Abstract: We study the Cauchy problem for the Benjamin--Ono--Zakharov--Kuznetsov equation on $\mathbb R^2$. Following the strategy of Kenig and Ziesler, we establish new maximal-function estimates adapted to the BO--ZK equation and use them to implement the Kenig--Koenig method. As a result, we improve the best previously known isotropic result of Nascimento (2020), lowering the local well-posedness threshold from $s>5/4$ to $s>19/16$. At the BO--ZK endpoint, the resulting isotropic data class also contains the anisotropic $E^{5/4+}$ class of the preceding theory. On bounded subsets of $H^s(\mathbb R^2)$, the lifespan may be chosen so that $

In particular, transverse dispersion compensates for the degeneration of longitudinal smoothing near the characteristic region. Together with refined short-time Strichartz estimates and a modified energy argument, these ingredients close the nonlinear estimates at the stated regularity. Existence, uniqueness, and continuous dependence on the initial data are then established in the corresponding solution class. The resulting threshold reflects the present optimization of the method and is not claimed to be sharp.

Our results provide the essential analytical tools required for the moving plane method in this setting. We prove first a weak comparison principle for $(-Δ)_p^s u = f(u)$ in bounded domains with sufficiently small measure, where only the boundedness of the weak solution is required. More importantly, we establish a strong comparison principle for continuous weak solutions in the parameter range $s \in (0, \frac{1}{2})$ and $\frac{1}{1-s} < p < 2$. Our proof introduces a localized barrier function and does not require any Hölder regularity of the weak solution, nor any smoothness of the domain. This presents a substantial contrast over previous study, which relied heavily on Hölder or even $C^{1,1}$ regularity. As a direct application, we employ these comparison principles to prove the symmetry of weak solutions to $(-Δ)_p^s u = f(u)$ under mild assumptions, which significantly extend existing symmetry theories for nonlocal quasilinear equations.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 本文显著扩展了散度自由函数同时逼近理论的应用范围，从光滑有界域延伸至整空间和一般无界域。所建立的逼近框架和推广的Lions-Magenes引理具有独立价值，为无界域上不可压缩流体方程的弱解能量等式问题提供了统一且完整的处理方案。

Abstract: We construct divergence-free, vector-valued approximation functions on the whole space $\mathbb{R}^d$, $d\geq 2$, as well as on general unbounded domains of uniform $\mathrm{C}^{1,1}$-type. These approximations converge simultaneously in both Sobolev and Lebesgue spaces. In the whole-space setting, we employ Bogovskiĭ\ operators to construct such approximations, thereby extending the approximation theory developed for smooth bounded domains and the simultaneous approximation framework introduced by \emph{Fefferman, Hajduk, and Robinson, {Proc. Lond. Math. Soc.} (3) \ {125} (2022), no.~4, 759-777}. As an application, we establish energy equality for Leray-Hopf weak solutions of the incompressible convective Brinkman-Forchheimer (CBF) equations on $\mathbb{R}^d$, $d\in\{2,3\}$ covering both the critical and supercritical regimes. For general unbounded domains, we employ the resolvent operator associated with the Stokes operator, developed by \emph{Farwig, Kozono and Sohr, {Acta Math.}, {195} (2005), 21-53}, to obtain simultaneous approximation results. We also establish a generalized version of the classical Lions-Magenes lemma, which is of independent interest. Finally, by combining this result with the simultaneous approximation framework for unbounded domains, we establish energy equality for weak solutions of the CBF equations on general unbounded domains.

### [82] [Sharp convergence rates for the vanishing discount problem with hyperbolic Aubry sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02779)
*Panrui Ni*

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Tonelli哈密顿系统中消失折扣问题的定量收敛率。当提升Aubry集为有限个双曲平衡点或双曲周期轨道之并时，证明了收敛率介于O(λ)与O(λ|log λ|)之间，并在特定条件下达到O(λ)。通过构造实例证明了两种收敛率均为最优，同时讨论了对退化和无限阶退化情形的下界与任意慢收敛。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Tonelli哈密顿系统的粘性解理论中，消失折扣问题（vanishing discount problem）关注当折扣因子λ趋于0时，解u_λ如何收敛到所选择的临界粘性解u_0。此前虽然已知u_λ→u_0成立，但缺乏系统的定量收敛率刻画。本文旨在建立该问题在一般Tonelli框架下的收敛速率理论。

Method: 假设提升Aubry集为有限个双曲构件（双曲平衡点或双曲周期轨道）的不交并。利用双曲动力系统的稳定流形结构，结合Mather测度的投影分析和粘性解的弱KAM理论，证明−Cλ≤u_λ−u_0≤Cλ|log λ|的估计；当每个构件对应的投影Mather测度下u_0均值为零时，将上界提升为O(λ)。同时构造退化（有限阶和无穷阶）例子以探索非双曲情形下的收敛速度下界。

Result: 主要结果为：(1)在双曲假设下建立了−Cλ≤u_λ−u_0≤Cλ|log λ|的收敛率；(2)在Mather测度均值条件成立时得到O(λ)收敛率；(3)证明当提升Aubry集为单一双曲平衡点或双曲周期轨道时收敛率为O(λ)；(4)通过例子说明O(λ)和O(λ|log λ|)均不可改进；(5)对r阶退化（r≥2）给出下界λ^{1/(2r-1)}，并构造无穷阶退化例子实现任意慢收敛。

Conclusion: 本文在Tonelli框架下首次建立了消失折扣问题的系统性定量理论，完整刻画了双曲情形下的收敛率（含最优性），并揭示了非双曲退化情形下收敛速度的本质放慢。这为后续研究更一般Aubry集结构的收敛率问题奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: Let $H\in C^2(T^*M)$ be a Tonelli Hamiltonian on a closed connected manifold and let $u_λ$ solve \[
  λu_λ+H(x,Du_λ)=c(H)\qquad\text{in }M. \] We study the convergence rate of $u_λ$ to the selected critical solution $u_0$. Assume that the lifted Aubry set is a finite union $\widetilde{A}=Γ_1\sqcup\cdots\sqcupΓ_N$, where each $Γ_i$ is either a hyperbolic equilibrium or a periodic orbit hyperbolic in the critical energy level. We prove \[
  -Cλ\le u_λ-u_0\le Cλ|\logλ|. \] Let $μ_i$ be the projected Mather measure associated with $Γ_i$. If \[
  \int_M u_0\,\mathrm dμ_i=0\qquad \text{for every }i, \]
  then \[
  \|u_λ-u_0\|_\infty\le Cλ. \] In particular, if the lifted Aubry set consists of a single hyperbolic equilibrium or a single hyperbolic periodic orbit, the convergence rate is $O(λ)$. We give examples showing that both convergence rates $O(λ)$ and $O(λ|\logλ|)$ are optimal. Without hyperbolicity, finite-order degenerate examples give lower bounds of order $λ^{1/(2r-1)}$ with $r\ge2$. We also construct infinite-order degenerate examples with arbitrarily slow convergence. Taken together, these results provide, to our knowledge, the first systematic quantitative theory for the vanishing discount problem in the Tonelli setting.

</details>

Motivation: 现有文献对于Kuznetsov和Westervelt型等非线性声学波动方程，已在二阶及更高阶Sobolev空间中建立了充分小初始数据下全局解的存在性，但尚不清楚能否在小性条件仅涉及一阶Sobolev空间的情形下建立全局可解性。这一开放性问题是驱动本研究的主要动机。

Method: 对一般粘性波动方程h(u,ut)utt=Δut+aΔu+f(u,ut,∇u,∇ut)施加光滑性假设，要求h(0,0)>0、f(0,0,0,0)=0且∇f(0,0,0,0)=0，并在W^{1,r}×W^{1,p}空间中分析全局解的存在性与解的指数衰减特性。

Result: 证明了在仅涉及一阶Sobolev空间的小性条件下，系统仍存在全局经典解，并且解对(u,ut)在W^{1,r}×W^{1,p}空间中具有指数衰减性。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，将该类一般粘性波动方程全局可解性的小性条件从高阶Sobolev空间放宽到一阶Sobolev空间是可行的，从而填补了该领域的一个开放性空白。

Abstract: In a smoothly bounded domain $\Om\subset\R^n$ with $n\geq 1$ and $a>0$, we consider an initial-boundary value problem for the general viscous wave equation \bas
  h(u,u_t) u_{tt} = \Del u_t + a\Del u + f(u,u_t,\na u,\na u_t) \eas which appears in models of nonlinear acoustics wave propagation; well-established equations of Kuznetsov and Westervelt type form particular examples.\abs %
While the existing literature offers extensive results on global solutions for sufficiently small initial data $(u_{0}, u_{0t})=(u, u_{t})|_{t=0}$ in second- and higher-order Sobolev spaces it appears to remain open how far global solvability can be established under smallness conditions involving only first-order Sobolev spaces. The present manuscript addresses this question by proving the existence of global classical solutions together with exponential decay of the pair $(u,u_{t})$ in $ W^{1,r}\times W^{1,p}$-Sobolev spaces whenever the nonlinearities $h$ and $f$ are sufficiently smooth and are such that $h(0,0)>0$ as well as $f(0,0,0,0)=0$ and $\na f(0,0,0,0)=0$.

</details>

Abstract: We prove the global existence and uniqueness of strong solutions to a constrained version of the damped nonlinear wave equation $$ \vartheta_{tt}+γ\vartheta_t-Δ\vartheta+|\vartheta|^{p-2}\vartheta=0 $$
  on a smooth bounded domain $\varOmega\subset\mathbb{R}^d$, where the evolution is projected onto the tangent space of the Hilbert manifold $$\mathcal{M} = \left\{ \vartheta\in L^2(\varOmega):\|\vartheta\|_{L^2(\varOmega)}=1 \right\}, $$
  which is the unit sphere in $L^2(\varOmega)$. We assume that
  $$p\in[2,\infty)\ \text{ for }\ d=1,2, \ \text{ while }\ 2\leq p\leq \frac{2(d-1)}{d-2} \ \text{ for }\ d\geq3.$$ By employing the Faedo--Galerkin approximation method, together with suitable a priori estimates and compactness arguments, we establish the global well-posedness of the problem. In particular, we show that the Hilbert manifold $\mathcal{M}$ is invariant under the flow, and hence the $L^2$-constraint is preserved throughout the evolution. Using the \emph{Lusternik--Schnirelmann theory}, we show that the corresponding stationary problem possesses at least countably many stationary solutions. We further investigate the long-time behaviour of solutions and prove that, along a subsequence, every solution of the constrained problem converges to a stationary solution by invoking \emph{Webb's theorem} and \emph{Barbalat's lemma}. When the initial data are sufficiently close to the first eigenfunction of the associated stationary problem, we show that the unique strong solution converges in $H_0^1(\varOmega)$ to the unique positive ground-state solution.

</details>

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究半凹函数的割迹（cut locus），通过接触方法引入其定义并证明与Lax-Oleinik半群定义的变分割迹一致。作者引入正则度R_φ量化C^{1,1}偏差，建立与割时间函数的定量关系，并由此证明共轭点在割迹之前不会出现。割点沿广义特征线全局传播，Alexandrov点沿校准曲线向前传播。此外证明割迹是Lebesgue零测集，并给出弱KAM解割迹封闭的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半凹函数的割迹在Hamilton-Jacobi方程理论和弱KAM理论中扮演核心角色，但已有定义依赖具体Hamiltonian且缺乏统一的解析刻画。作者希望建立不依赖于Hamiltonian的割迹定义（通过接触方法），并给出割迹的正则性与传播性质的定量刻画。

Method: 采用"接触"（touching）方法定义割迹，以近次微分（proximal subdifferential）为空作为解析判据。引入正则度R_φ度量C^{1,1}偏差，建立与割时间函数τ_{φ,H}的点态定量关系。利用该关系导出校准曲线的分离估计，进而证明沿广义特征线的传播性质。对切割时间与割迹封闭性给出必要充分条件。

Result: 证明了接触定义下的割迹与Lax-Oleinik半群定义的变分割迹在任意Hamiltonian下一致；建立了R_φ(x)~1/τ_{φ,H}(x)的定量关系及显式截断常数；证明校准曲线在割迹前无共轭点；割点沿广义特征线全局传播且Alexandrov点沿校准曲线传播，二阶导数满足矩阵Riccati方程；割迹为Lebesgue零测集；给出了弱KAM解割迹封闭的充要条件。

Conclusion: 本文建立了半凹函数割迹的统一理论框架，将几何割迹与动力学割迹（基于Lax-Oleinik半群）统一起来，并通过正则度R_φ给出了割迹相关的精细定量估计和传播规律，为Hamilton-Jacobi方程和弱KAM理论提供了有力的分析工具。

Abstract: For a semiconcave function with linear modulus, we introduce the cut locus through a touching approach and prove that it coincides with the variational cut locus defined via the Lax--Oleinik semigroup, independently of the Hamiltonian. Cut points are characterized by the emptiness of the proximal subdifferential of $φ$, which we use as the analytic criterion behind the touching definition. We introduce the degree of regularity $R_φ$, which measures the $C^{1,1}$ deviation of $φ$, and prove the quantitative relation $R_φ(x)\sim 1/τ_{φ,H}(x)$ with the cut time function, valid pointwise up to explicit truncation constants. From this relation we derive a separation estimate for calibrated curves, showing that no conjugate points occur before the cut locus. This estimate supplies the tools for the propagation results. Cut points propagate globally along generalized characteristics for the evolutionary Hamilton--Jacobi equation, and Alexandrov points propagate forward along calibrated curves, with the second derivative satisfying a matrix Riccati equation. We also show that the cut locus is a Lebesgue null set and give a streamlined proof of Alexandrov's theorem. Finally, we give necessary and sufficient conditions for the cut locus of a weak KAM solution to be closed, in terms of the cut time function, the $C^{1,1}$ support, and the degree of regularity.

</details>

### [86] [Folded-Algebraic Matroids: Characteristic Rigidity and Almost-Entropic Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01664)
*Shahram Khazaei*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入"折叠代数拟阵"（folded-algebraic matroids）这一新的表示类：每个拟阵元素替换为有限代数量元组，经一次均匀缩放后超越度与拟阵秩一致。该类包含代数拟阵和折叠线性拟阵，并包含于几乎熵拟阵类（秩函数为缩放熵函数的极限）中。作者证明该包含关系是真包含，并证明 Gordon 的秩三拟阵 M(p) 恰在特征为 p 的域上有折叠代数表示。通过保持几乎熵性质的"点识别"构造，得到 13 元秩三 3-连通拟阵 C_{2,3}，它几乎熵但非折叠代数；以公共元素为 dealer 还可得到具有不相容特征要求的 12 参与者连通端口。最后给出多个表示类间分离区域的紧凑显式见证与尺寸界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟阵表示理论中长期关注不同表示类（代数、线性、熵）之间的关系。代数拟阵与折叠线性拟阵是两类不同来源的经典对象，而几乎熵拟阵刻画了信息论中熵函数极限可达的秩行为。作者旨在统一并厘清这些类之间的包含关系，特别希望找到能区分"几乎熵"与"代数/折叠线性"的具体有限拟阵例子，并回答 Gordon 经典拟阵 M(p) 在何种特征域上可表示的代数问题。

Method: 作者提出"折叠代数拟阵"概念，将每个元素映射为有限代数量元组并经一次均匀缩放使超越度匹配秩。利用代数几何与域论方法证明其包含关系：代数型与折叠线型均折叠代数，折叠代数包含于几乎熵。关键构造工具是"点识别"（point-identification）操作，该操作保持几乎熵性质，用于制造 13 元素秩三 3-连通拟阵 C_{2,3}。对 Gordon 拟阵 M(p)，通过特征域敏感性证明其在特征 p 域上恰有折叠代数表示。还构建了 12 参与者端口及多个分离区域的显式紧凑见证。

Result: (1) 折叠代数拟阵类真包含于几乎熵拟阵类，即存在拟阵几乎熵但非折叠代数；(2) Gordon 的秩三拟阵 M(p) 在域 K 上有折叠代数表示当且仅当 char(K)=p；(3) 构造了 13 元秩三 3-连通拟阵 C_{2,3}，几乎熵但非折叠代数；(4) 构造了 12 参与者连通端口，其 dealer 为公共元素，但具有不相容的特征要求；(5) 给出了多个表示类之间分离区域的紧凑显式见证和尺寸上界。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个新的拟阵表示层级，统一了代数与线性折叠类，并精确刻画了与几乎熵类的边界：真包含且存在分离性例子。Gordon 拟阵在不同特征域上的可表示性为折叠代数表示提供了强有力的特征不变量。C_{2,3} 和 12 参与者端口等构造表明折叠代数与几乎熵类在实际规模上即可区分，为信息论与组合学交叉领域的拟阵分类提供了新的判据与工具。

Abstract: We introduce folded-algebraic matroids. In such a representation, every matroid element is replaced by a finite tuple of algebraic quantities, and transcendence degree agrees with matroid rank after one uniform scaling. The resulting class contains both algebraic and folded-linear matroids and is contained in the class of almost-entropic matroids, whose rank functions are limits of scaled entropy functions. We prove that the latter containment is proper. Our main result concerns the classical rank-three matroids $M(p)$ of Gordon. For every prime $p$, we show that $M(p)$ has a folded-algebraic representation over a field $K$ if and only if $K$ has characteristic $p$. We then use a point-identification construction that preserves almost-entropicity to obtain a $13$-element rank-three $3$-connected matroid $C_{2,3}$ that is almost entropic but not folded algebraic. Choosing a common element as dealer also yields a connected $12$-participant port with incompatible characteristic requirements. Finally, we record compact explicit witnesses and size bounds for several other separating regions among the representation classes.

</details>

*Tony Newton*

TL;DR: 本文研究了带超端口（superport）电网的响应矩阵的任意子行列式（minors），填补了此前仅给出单个响应项和整体行列式森林公式的空白，给出了每个贡献生成森林所携带的符号规则——即通过约化商端口图的关联子式χ̂_F(I) ∈ {0,±1} 来确定系数，最终得到任意响应子式是一个带符号的加权生成森林求和，且非零的充要条件为补集边形成该商图的生成树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已为带超端口网络的响应矩阵给出了单个响应项和整体行列式的森林公式，但缺少任意子行列式的情况。该缺失情形的困难在于不仅需要知道哪些生成森林贡献，还需确定每个森林贡献的符号，这正是本文要解决的问题。

Method: 论文通过选取每个超端口中的参考顶点，将响应矩阵表示为L=(D^T K^{-1} D)^{-1}。对物理生成森林F，通过收缩其分量得到更小的商端口图H_F，其约化关联矩阵B_F的列仅具有0、±e_a、e_a-e_b三种形式，因此所有方阵关联子式均为0或±1。将选择器行E_I^T拼接到B_F之上构造完成商关联行列式χ̂_F(I)，证明其可通过块三角化简直接得到χ̂_F(I)∈{0,±1}，其非零恰当互补商边集N\I形成H_F的生成树时成立。任意响应子式即为加权生成森林和，系数为χ̂_F(I)χ̂_F(J)，Jacobi互补子式因子在最终定理中消去。

Result: 给出了任意响应子式的完整带符号生成森林公式，其中每个森林贡献的系数由商端口图关联子式的乘积χ̂_F(I)χ̂_F(J)确定；χ̂_F(I)取值仅为0或±1，非零的充要条件是补集商边构成该商图的生成树。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了超端口网络响应矩阵子行列式的空白，确立了完整的带符号森林求和公式，使响应矩阵任意子式的计算与符号问题得到统一解决。

Abstract: An electrical network can be summarized at its boundary by a response matrix: prescribed boundary voltages determine boundary currents. A superport network adds a constraint by grouping boundary terminals into superports, requiring the total current in each group to be zero and making voltage differences inside the groups the natural coordinates. Earlier work determined forest formulas for a single response entry and for the determinant of the whole response matrix. The missing case was an arbitrary subdeterminant, or minor: one needs to know not only which spanning forests contribute, but also the sign carried by each forest. This paper supplies that sign rule. After choosing one reference vertex in each superport, the response is \[ L=\left(D^{T}K^{-1}D\right)^{-1}, \] with \(K\) the grounded weighted Laplacian and \(D\) recording the selected voltage differences. Contracting the components of a physical spanning forest \(F\) produces a much smaller quotient port graph \(H_F\). Its reduced incidence matrix \[ B_F=Q_FD \] has columns only of the forms \[ 0,\qquad \pm e_a,\qquad e_a-e_b. \] Hence every square incidence minor is exactly \(0\) or \(\pm1\). For a \(k\)-set of response coordinates \(I\), append to \(B_F\) the selector rows \(E_I^{T}\) and define the completed quotient-incidence determinant \[ \widehatχ_F(I) = \det \begin{pmatrix} B_F\\ E_I^{T} \end{pmatrix}. \] For coordinate sets \(I,J\) of the same size, the arbitrary response minor is a weighted spanning-forest sum whose coefficient is simply \[ \widehatχ_F(I)\widehatχ_F(J). \] Thus the Jacobi complementary-minor factors used in the derivation disappear from the final theorem. Direct block-triangular reduction gives \[ \widehatχ_F(I)\in\{0,\pm1\}, \] with nonvanishing occurring exactly when the complementary quotient edges \(N\setminus I\) form a spanning tree of \(H_F\).

</details>
